So c5_vec_fft:
L = 1 << (2*n -1).bit_length()
Fh = fft(h, L)
Fg = fft((1-h)[::-1], L)
corr = ifft(Fh*Fg).real[:2*n-1]
return corr*dx.
This is O(n log n). For n up to 8192, fast. Need ensure numerical small negative; max ok. But objective exact? The evaluator likely uses np.correlate direct; FFT has floating error ~1e-15, okay. Use projection:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=1e-14: break
          h += diff*room/tot
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<=1e-14: break
          h += diff*h/tot
    # final exact fix: find positions not at bounds and adjust one?
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1-1e-12)[0]; else idx=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
       if len(idx): h[idx[0]] += diff
    return clip
```
This may make slight sum exact but one point out of bounds if diff large? But after iterative diff tiny. Use `np.clip` after final, recenter? We can do closed-form? For small diff, fine.

`compute_c5_fft_vec` returns c * dx. Need handle hermitian real. Use `np.fft.rfft` for speed?
```
def compute_c5_fft_vec(h,n):
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L*=2
    fh=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
    fhr=np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1],L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fh*fhr,L)[:2*n-1]
    return c*(2.0/n)
```
For many evals, computing L and fft allocate. Could cache L and basis? For a given n, we can precompute L and maybe use `np.fft.rfft`. Simpler.

`add_lag_gradient(g,h,m,w,n)`:
```
k = m - (n-1)
if w ==0: return
nv = n - abs(k)
if k >=0:
   # c=sum h[n+k](1-h[n])
   g[k:n] += w*(1.0 - h[0:nv])
   g[0:nv] += -w*h[k:n]
else:
   q=-k; nv=n-q
   # c=sum h[n](1-h[n+q])
   g[0:nv] += w*(1.0 - h[q:n])
   g[q:n] += -w*h[0:nv]
```
Check p=0 h=[0,0,0], active lag -2 (m=0) w=1, q=2,nv=1: g[0:1]+=1-h[2:3]=1, g[2:3]+=-h[0:1]=0. Gradient at h0 for c_{-2}: derivative of c= h0(1-h2) wrt h0=1 yes. For active lag +2 (m=4), k=2,nv=1: g[2:3]+=1-h[0:1]=1 (pos2 derivative wrt h2?), c_{+2}=h2(1-h0) wrt h0 = -h2=0? Wait derivative wrt h0 should be -h2=0. Our g[0:1]+=-w*h[2:3]=0; g[2:3]+=(1-h0)=1. Good.
For p=1 lag -2? nv=1, positions g[0] and g[2]; p=1 not affected; indeed c_{-2}=h0(1-h2) no h1. Good.

```
def fast_pair_local(h,n,deadline,K=12,random_pairs=20,max_stalls=3,step_fracs=None):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_c5_vec(h,n) # use fft? For exact maintained, start direct? Use compute_c5_fft_vec, recompute direct later.
    best_c=c.max()
    # maybe direct exact initial to avoid FFT round: if n<=2048 use np.correlate,dx
    c=compute_c5_vec_direct(h,n)
    best_c=...
    it=0; stall=0
    while time < deadline:
       active= lag_active(c, K=...): We want lags with c >= best_c - tol. tol = max(1e-8, 1e-4*best_c)? But maybe many lags if flat. Limit top 20. Use top `top_lags(c, 16, relative=0.005)`.
       if len(active)==0: active=[argmax]
       g=zeros; for m in active: w=1/len(active) (or softmax). Better use softmax weights to focus highest:
          vals=c[active]; alpha=50? We can compute weights=softmax(alpha*(vals-best_c))? Use uniform to avoid overfocus? For coordinate, uniform top K.
       # add gradient
       for m,w in zip(active,weights): add_lag_gradient(g,h,m,w,n)
       # optional normalize? g scale doesn't matter for top selection but gain differences do.
       # Candidate positions:
       high = indices where h>1e-8, choose top by g. But gradient may high at 0? To decrease requires room h>0. Low = where h<1-1e-8 choose bottom by g.
       # If gradient all same? fallback random.
       high_idx = np.argsort(g) reversed? We want high g for i (decrease). `high = np.where(h>1e-6)[0]`; take top min(12, len) by g[high]. low = np.where(h<1-1e-6)[0]; take bottom 12 by g[low].
       # Also include positions with max c contributions? not.
       candidates=[]
       for i in high: for j in low: if i!=j append (g[i]-g[j], i,j)
       sort desc gain; take top C=120.
       # random candidates: sample 20 high/low random.
       best_trial=best_c; best_move=None
       for gain,i,j in candidates:
           maxamt = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
           if maxamt <= 1e-8: continue
           # choose dt list: fractions of maxamt: [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.4?]. But if maxamt huge (0.5), full may be too big. We can also use absolute steps [0.05,0.01,0.005,0.001] clipped.
           for amt in candidate_amounts(h[i],1-h[j]):
               trial = evaluate_pair(c,h,i,j,amt,n)
               if trial < best_trial - 1e-13:
                   best_trial=trial; best_move=(i,j,amt,best_gain)
                   if best_trial < best_c - 1e-10: break maybe not, but still check other amt? We just want best overall.
       if best_move:
           apply_pair_move_to_c(h,c,i,j,amt,n) # updates h,c
           best_c=best_trial
           stall=0
       else:
           stall +=1
           # try random full coordinate desc with small steps for a while
           if not random_lowercase: ...
       it++
    # final recompute direct
```

Apply_pair_move_to_c:
```
def apply_pair_move_to_c(h,c,i,j,amt,n):
   L=2n-1; center=n-1
   add_linear(c,h,i,-amt,n)
   add_linear(c,h,j, amt,n)
   if i!=j:
      D=abs(i-j)
      c[center] -= 2*amt*amt
      c[center+D] += amt*amt
      c[center-D] += amt*amt
   h[i]-=amt; h[j]+=amt
   # clip tiny
   h[i]=min(max(h[i],0),1); h[j]=...
   # sum preserved; if clip, adjust? amt chosen <= bounds so no clip needed except fp.
```

But if we run fast_pair on n=1024 for 500s, we can improve. Let's implement efficient candidate amount list based on current h:
```
def move_amounts(hi, hj_room):
    maxamt = min(hi, hj_room)
    if maxamt <= 1e-12: return []
    vals = set()
    for f in (1,0.75,0.5,0.25,0.1):
        vals.add(min(maxamt, f*maxamt))
    for a in (0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005):
        if a < maxamt: vals.add(a)
    # Sort descending? For early break? We'll just evaluate all maybe.
    return sorted(vals, reverse=True)
```

           maxit=...
           res=minimize(smooth_topk_obj, best_h, jac=smooth_topk_grad, args=(n, active, alpha, 0.01), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options...)

           c_new=compute_c5_direct? maybe fft

       # if no improvement break

Function `fast_pair_local(h,n,deadline,verbose=False)`:
Inputs h maybe length n. Steps:
```
h = project_feasible(h,n).astype(float)
c = compute_c5_direct(h)  # for exact
best_c = float(c.max())
move_count=0; stalls=0
step_sizes = [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005,0.00002,0.00001]
while time.time() < deadline:
    if c.max() < best_c: best_c = c.max(); if c.max()>best_c? recompute? We maintain.
    # maybe ensure c max equals direct every 100 moves
    if move_count % 25 == 0:
       c_direct = compute_c5_direct(h)
       if c_direct.max() < best_c: best_c = c_direct.max()
       c = c_direct
       if best_c < target? not.
    active = active_lags(c, 16, 0.004)
    weights? If len active <2, add second highest? use top at least 1.
    g = np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active:
        w = 1.0/len(active)  # or softmax
        add_lag_gradient(g,h,idx,w,n)
    # If gradient zeros (c flat? constant h) break.
    # Candidate high/low:
    pos_room = np.where(h > 1e-7)[0]
    neg_room = np.where(h < 1 - 1e-7)[0]
    if len(pos_room)==0 or len(neg_room)==0: break
    high = pos_room[np.argsort(g[pos_room])[::-1]][:12]
    low = neg_room[np.argsort(g[neg_room])[:12]]
    # If no negative lower than some, include random.
    cand=[]
    for i in high:
       for j in low:
          if i != j:
             cand.append((g[i]-g[j], i, j))
    # Add reverse? If g low negative etc, high-low positive. Some candidates may gain <=0; still include? Only if > small? We can include all top.
    cand.sort(reverse=True)
    # Also random 20 pairs among room
    for _ in range(20):
        i = pos_room[rng.randint(len(pos_room))]; j = neg_room[...]
        if i != j: cand.append((g[i]-g[j], i,j))
    # Choose top 60 unique (i,j)
    seen=set? To avoid set overhead? Use list.
    top_cand = cand[:60]
    chosen=None; chosen_trial=best_c
    # amount candidates maybe global step list filtered by maxamt
    for gain,i,j in top_cand:
        maxamt = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
        if maxamt <= 1e-8: continue
        # Generate amts: use base min(0.02,maxamt), plus global small, plus fractions
        amts=[]
        base = min(0.02, maxamt)
        amts.append(base)
        for f in (2.0,0.5,0.25):

            if a < maxamt: amts.append(a)
        # deduplicate by rounding maybe not necessary; skip if <=current best?

                # if very good, break amts

        if time.time() > deadline-1: break
    if chosen:
        apply_pair_move(h,c,chosen[0],chosen[1],chosen[2],n)

        if chosen_trial < best_c: best_c=chosen_trial
        move_count +=1; stalls=0

        stalls +=1

           # perform random acceptance? Or break after some random attempts
           # Try 200 random pairs with small amts using eval.
           improved=False

           if improved: stalls=0
           else: break
```

`active_lags(c, top_k, rel)`:
```
mx = c.max()
th = mx - max(1e-10, rel*mx)
idx = np.where(c >= th)[0]
if len(idx) == 0: idx = [np.argmax(c)]
if len(idx) > top_k:
    order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:top_k]
    idx = idx[order]
return idx
```

Function list in final code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

FOURIER_B = None
...

def next_fast_len(m):
    L=1
    while L < m: L <<= 1
    return L

def compute_c5_direct(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * dx

def compute_c5_fft_vec(h, L=None):
    n=len(h)
    if L is None: L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    fg = np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1], L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fh*fg, L)[:2*n-1]
    return c * (2.0/n)
```
Maybe `irfft` output length L could have tiny imaginary? real.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += diff*room/tot
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += diff*h/tot
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
           idx=np.where(h<1.0-1e-12)[0]
       else: idx=np.where(h>1e-12)[0]
       if len(idx)>0:
           h[idx[0]] += diff
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If final correction makes a value out of bounds by >1e-12, clip changes sum. But diff tiny. Maybe After final, ensure sum with another tiny distribute? Could loop one more:
```
for _ in range(3): diff=...; distribute to interior not bounds
```
Add final small distribution.

`active_lags(c, top_k=16, rel=0.002)`:
```
def active_lags(c, top_k=16, rel=0.002):
    mx=float(c.max())
    th=mx - max(1e-10, rel*mx)
    idx=np.where(c >= th)[0]
    if idx.size==0: return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if idx.size > top_k:
       order=np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:top_k]
       idx=idx[order]
    return idx.astype(int)
```

`add_lag_gradient(g,h,m,w,n)`:
```
def add_lag_gradient(g,h,m,w,n):
    if w == 0.0: return
    k=m-(n-1)
    if k>=0:
       q=k; nv=n-q
       if nv<=0: return
       g[q:n] += w*(1.0-h[0:nv])
       g[0:nv] += -w*h[q:n]
    else:
       q=-k; nv=n-q
       if nv<=0: return
       g[0:nv] += w*(1.0-h[q:n])
       g[q:n] += -w*h[0:nv]
```
Check k=0: in q=0, g[0:n]+=w*(1-h[0:n]), g[0:n]+= -w*h[0:n] -> g += w*(1-2h). Good. Slides same, okay. But q=0, h[q:n] length n, h[0:nv] length n; both. Good.

`add_linear_effect(c,h,p,delta,n)`:
```
def add_linear_effect(c,h,p,delta,n):
    if delta == 0.0: return
    center=n-1
    c[center] += delta*(1.0-2.0*h[p])
    if p>0:
       c[center+1:center+1+p] += delta*(1.0-h[p-1::-1])
       c[center-p:center] += -delta*h[0:p]
    if n-p-1>0:
       c[center+1:center+n-p] += -delta*h[p+1:n]
       c[p:center] += delta*(1.0-h[n-1:p:-1])
```
Need ensure slice indices correct when p=0: center+1:center+n-p = center+1:center+n = n:2n-1 length n-1, but we only want n-p-1=n-1? yes indices n..2n-2? center=n-1, stop center+n =2n-1, exclusive, length n-1? For n=3 center=2 stop 5, indices 3,4 length2. Good.
For negative T1 c[p:center], if p=0 c[0:center] length n-1; values h[n-1:0:-1] length n-1, good.
For p=n-1 positive T1 c[center+1:center+n-p] = center+1:center+1 = none; negative T2 c[center-p:center] = c[0:center] lengths? p=n-1, center-p=n-1-(n-1)=0, length n-1, good; positive T1 p>0 c[center+1:center+1+p]=c[n:n-1+n-1]=c[n:2n-2] length n-1, values h[n-2::-1] length n-1. Good.
Quadratic pair:
```
def add_pair_quadratic(c,i,j,amt,n):
    D=abs(i-j)
    amt2=amt*amt
    center=n-1
    c[center] -= 2*amt2
    if D>0:
       c[center+D] += amt2
       c[center-D] += amt2
```
Check indices: for D>0 and D≤n-1, center±D within 0..2n-2. Good.

`eval_pair_move(c,h,i,j,amt,n)`:
```
def eval_pair_move(c,h,i,j,amt,n):
    if i==j or amt<=0: return c.max()
    c2=c.copy()
    add_linear_effect(c2,h,i,-amt,n)
    add_linear_effect(c2,h,j,amt,n)
    # quad
    D=abs(i-j); amt2=amt*amt; center=n-1
    c2[center] -= 2*amt2
    c2[center+D] += amt2
    c2[center-D] += amt2
    return float(c2.max())
```
But wait, for pair i changed -amt, j +amt. If i and j are the same? not. The quadratic self terms: for i self -amt² center; for j self -amt² center -> -2. Cross terms: -δiδj at lag i-j and j-i = +amt². Good. Does `add_linear_effect` with δ for i and j include their mutual linear using old h, which is correct. Good.

`apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,amt,n)`:
```
def apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,amt,n):
    i=int(i); j=int(j)
    add_linear_effect(c,h,i,-amt,n)
    add_linear_effect(c,h,j,amt,n)
    D=abs(i-j); amt2=amt*amt; center=n-1
    c[center] -= 2*amt2
    if D>0:
       c[center+D] += amt2; c[center-D] += amt2
    h[i] = max(0.0,min(1.0,h[i]-amt))
    h[j] = max(0.0,min(1.0,h[j]+amt))
```
If clip occurred due fp, sum not exact. We can recompute and project later.

```
def fast_pair_local(h,n,deadline,seed=1,top_k=16,cand_count=80,step0=0.05):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_c5_direct(h)
    best_c=float(c.max())
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+n)
    step=step0
    stale=0
    moves=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
        if moves % 15 == 0:
            c_dir=compute_c5_direct(h)
            c=c_dir
            true_c=float(c.max())
            if true_c < best_c: best_c=true_c
            elif true_c > best_c + 1e-8: best_c=true_c  # safeguard
        active=active_lags(c, top_k, 0.002)
        if active.size < 1: active=np.array([np.argmax(c)])
        vals=c[active]
        mx=vals.max()
        # weights softmax alpha 20
        sh=20*(vals-mx)
        w=np.exp(sh); w=w/w.sum()
        g=np.zeros(n)
        for idx,wi in zip(active,w):
            if wi > 1e-14:
                add_lag_gradient(g,h,idx,wi,n)
        room_pos=np.where(h>1e-8)[0]
        room_neg=np.where(h<1-1e-8)[0]
        if room_pos.size<1 or room_neg.size<1:
            break
        # choose counts maybe 15
        ncand = min(15, room_pos.size, room_neg.size)
        high=room_pos[np.argsort(g[room_pos])[::-1]][:ncand]
        low=room_neg[np.argsort(g[room_neg])[:ncand]]
        cand=[]; seen=set()
        for ii in high:
           gi=g[ii]
           for jj in low:
              if ii==jj: continue
              key=ii*n+jj
              if key not in seen:
                 seen.add(key)
                 cand.append((gi-g[jj], ii, jj))
        # random candidates
        for _ in range(20):
           ii=room_pos[rng.randint(room_pos.size)]; jj=room_neg[rng.randint(room_neg.size)]
           if ii==jj: continue
           key=ii*n+jj
           if key not in seen:
              seen.add(key); cand.append((g[ii]-g[jj], ii, jj))
        if not cand: break
        if cand_count:
           cand.sort(key=lambda? no lambdas! Can't use lambda. We can use `cand.sort(reverse=True)` because tuples first element gain. Yes no lambda.
           cand=cand[:cand_count]
        chosen=None; chosen_c=best_c
        # amounts based on step
        amts_multipliers = (2.0,1.0,0.5)
        for _, ii, jj in cand:
           maxamt = min(h[ii], 1.0-h[jj])
           if maxamt <= 1e-9: continue
           for mult in amts_multipliers:
               amt = maxamt if mult*step > maxamt else mult*step
               if amt <= 1e-10: continue
               trial = eval_pair_move(c,h,ii,jj,amt,n)
               if trial < chosen_c - 1e-13:
                   chosen_c=trial; chosen=(ii,jj,amt)
                   if chosen_c < best_c - 1e-5:
                       break
           if time.time() > deadline - 2:
               break
        if chosen is not None:
           apply_pair_move(h,c,chosen[0],chosen[1],chosen[2],n)
           if chosen_c < best_c:
               best_c=float(c.max()) # after update exact maybe
           moves +=1; stale=0
        else:
           # random trial loop with one step
           improved=False
           for _ in range(200):
              if time.time()>deadline-2: break
              ii=room_pos[rng.randint(room_pos.size)]; jj=room_neg[rng.randint(room_neg.size)]
              if ii==jj: continue
              maxamt=min(h[ii],1-h[jj]); amt=min(maxamt, step)
              if amt<=1e-9: continue
              trial=eval_pair_move(c,h,ii,jj,amt,n)
              if trial < best_c - 1e-13:
                  apply_pair_move(h,c,ii,jj,amt,n)
                  best_c=float(c.max()); moves+=1; improved=True; break
           if improved:
              stale=0
           else:
              step *= 0.5
              stale +=1
              if stale >= 3:
                 # try lbfgs escape if time > 40
                 if time.time() < deadline - 60:
                    t_esc = min(deadline, time.time()+60)
                    h2,c2 = run_active_lbfgs(h,n,t_esc,top_k=20,maxiter=300)
                    if c2 < best_c:
                       best_h? Need run_active returns new h,c; update local variables. But fast_pair_local local h/c. We'll update and recompute c.
                      h=project_feasible(h2,n); c=compute_c5_direct(h); best_c=float(c.max())
                    # reset step maybe 0.02

                    stale=0

def run_active_lbfgs(h,n,deadline, top_k=16, maxiter=400):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=compute_c5_fft_vec(h) maybe direct? Start best_c=direct? Use fft for speed.
    best_c=float(np.max(c))
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    configs = [(top_k, 20, 200), (top_k, 200, 300), (min(40, top_k*3), 2000, 300), (min(60, top_k*5), 20000, 200)]
    for tk,alpha,itmax in configs:
       rem=deadline-time
       if rem<10: break
       c_now=compute_c5_fft_vec(h)
       active=active_lags(c_now, tk, 0.01)
       mu=0.05 if n large? use 0.01
       maxit=min(itmax, max(50, int(rem*2)))
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_topk_obj, h, jac=smooth_topk_grad, args=(n, active, alpha, 0.01), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-14,'maxfun':maxit*8,'maxcor':60})
          h_new=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          c_new=compute_c5_fft_vec(h_new)
          newc=float(c_new.max())
          if newc < best_c - 1e-14:
             h=h_new.copy(); best_c=newc
       except: pass
    # final direct maybe if time
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=compute_c5_fft_vec(h) # direct? L-BFGS start/return for local maybe use direct if n<=2048 and time.
    best_c=float(c.max())
    return h,best_c
```

`smooth_topk_obj` uses FFT c. active fixed. Need if c[active] all same and alpha high, logsumexp stable. Implement:
```
def smooth_topk_obj(x,n,active,alpha,mu):
    c=compute_c5_fft_vec(x)
    vals=c[active]
    mx=vals.max()
    sh=alpha*(vals-mx)
    smx=sh.max()
    if smx>400: val=mx+(smx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh-smx))))/alpha
    else: val=mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh)))/alpha
    if mu>0:
       diff=x.sum()-0.5*n; val += mu*diff*diff
    return float(val)
```
`smooth_topk_grad` similar and gradient. Use stable weights:
```
sh=alpha*(vals-mx); smx=sh.max(); w=np.exp(sh-smx); w/=w.sum()
grad=zeros; for sidx,m in enumerate(active): add_lag_gradient(grad,x,m,w[sidx],n)
if mu: grad += 2*mu*(x.sum()-n/2)
```
No direct c in gradient? uses same c.

`active` should be numpy array. In objective, if active empty, return 0.5? Not.

Potential issue: L-BFGS-B with bounds and equality penalty: It may find h with sum slightly not n/2; projection after. Good.

Now, candidate generation in run: Need define `make_upsampled` and `make_random_binary`, `make_beatty`.

`make_upsampled(h_old,n_new)`:
```
def make_upsampled(h_old,n_new):
    n_old=len(h_old)
    if n_old==n_new: return h_old.copy()
    old_x=np.arange(n_old, dtype=float)*(2.0/n_old)
    new_x=np.arange(n_new, dtype=float)*(2.0/n_new)
    # step interpolation: idx=np.searchsorted(old_x, new_x, side='right')-1
    idx=np.floor(new_x/(2.0/n_old)).astype(int)
    idx=np.clip(idx,0,n_old-1)
    h=h_old[idx]
    return project_feasible(h,n_new)
```
`np.searchsorted` maybe. Good.

`make_random_binary(n,rng)`: exactly n//2 ones if n even? For n not even? Our n even. If n odd, target n/2 not integer; but we choose even. Use `n//2` ones and one point fractional if n odd. For even exact.
```
def make_random_binary(n,rng):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   k=n//2
   idx=rng.choice(n,k,replace=False)
   h[idx]=1
   rem=0.5*n-h.sum()
   if abs(rem)>1e-12:
      zeros=np.where(h<1)[0]
      if len(zeros): h[zeros[0]] += rem
   return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n odd, k=n//2, rem=0.5, add to a zero fractional 0.5. Good.

def run_fourier_search(seed, start_time, deadline):
    prepare_fourier_basis(2048,2)
    D=2*FOURIER_F+3
    bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]*D + [(0.2,1.0)]
    bestx=None; bestc=1.0
    try:
       # maybe initial guess
       x0 = [0.8,0.0,0.0,0.3,0.1,0.0,0.0,0.4]? length D? D=7? For F=2, D=7. Let's not specify.
       res = differential_evolution(fourier_obj, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=12, popsize=8, polish=False, tol=1e-5, updating='deferred', init='soo')
       if res.fun < bestc: bestx=res.x; bestc=res.fun
    except Exception:
       # random starts NM
       gsrng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
       for r in range(5):
           x0=gsrng.uniform(-1,1,size=D+1)
           x0[-1]=gsrng.uniform(0.2,1)
           try: res=minimize(fourier_obj,x0,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':300,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-8})
           ...
    # NM polish bestx
    if bestx is not None:
       try: res=minimize(fourier_obj,bestx,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':500,'xatol':1e-6,'fatol':1e-9}); bestx=res.x; bestc=res.fun
       except: pass
    return bestx
```

`build_fourier_h(params,n)`:
```
def build_fourier_h(params,n):
    F=FOURIER_F if FOURIER_F is not None else 2
    D=2*F+3
    x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    B=np.zeros((n,D))... same; center
    coeffs = np.asarray(params[:D],float)
    A=float(params[D]); clip [0,1]
    f=B@coeffs
    maxf=np.max(np.abs(f))
    if maxf<1e-12: f=np.cos(np.pi*x)-mean; maxf=1
    h=0.5 + A*(f/maxf)
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Could reuse global if n==FOURIER_N, but okay. Need avoid code duplication? Write helper `build_fourier_basis(n,F)` returning B, fallback. But no closures. Top-level.
```

def prepare_fourier_basis(n,F): global... B,fb=build...
def build_fourier_h(params,n): B,fb=build_fourier_basis(n,FOURIER_F) ...
```

Detailed `run_active_lbfgs`:
```
def run_active_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, top_k=16, mu=0.05):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).astype(float)
    c = compute_c5_direct(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    configs = [
       (top_k, 20.0, mu, 250),
       (top_k, 200.0, mu, 300),
       (min(40, top_k*2), 2000.0, 0.02, 300),
       (min(80, top_k*3), 20000.0, 0.01, 200),
    ]
    for tk, alpha, mu_i, itmax in configs:
       rem = deadline - time.time()
       if rem < 15: break
       c_now = compute_c5_fft_vec(best_h)
       active = active_lags(c_now, tk, 0.01)
       maxit = min(itmax, max(30, int(rem * 2)))
       try:
          res = minimize(smooth_topk_obj, best_h, jac=smooth_topk_grad,
                         args=(n, active, alpha, mu_i), method='L-BFGS-B',
                         bounds=bounds,
                         options={'maxiter': maxit, 'ftol': 1e-20, 'gtol': 1e-16,
                                  'maxfun': maxit * 10, 'maxcor': 60, 'maxls': 60})
          h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
          c_new = compute_c5_direct(h_new)
          new_c = float(c_new.max())
          if new_c < best_c - 1e-15:
              best_c = new_c
              best_h = h_new
       except Exception:
          pass
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
    return best_h, float(c.max())
```
Maybe if new_c equal but feasible, accept? Not needed.

```

    key=n
    if key not in FFT_L_CACHE: FFT_L_CACHE[key]=next_fast_len(2*n-1)

```

Let's write `project_feasible` robust:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14: break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14: break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
        if idx.size == 0: break
        # distribute over all idx equally to avoid one point big
        h[idx] += diff / idx.size
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
If distributing equally, after clip maybe sum still off; loops. If diff huge due initial far, may fail but not.

`run_fourier_search`:
```
def run_fourier_search(seed):
    prepare_fourier_basis(2048, 2)
    F=FOURIER_F; D=2*F+3
    bounds = [(-1.0,1.0)]*D + [(0.25,1.0)]
    bestx = None
    x = np.random.RandomState(seed).uniform(-1.0,1.0,size=D+1)
    x[-1]=0.5
    bestx = x; bestc=fourier_obj(x)
    try:
       res = differential_evolution(fourier_obj, bounds, seed=seed, maxiter=10, popsize=8, polish=False, tol=1e-4, updating='deferred', init='latinhypercube')
       if bestx is None or res.fun < bestc:
           bestx=res.x; bestc=res.fun
    except Exception:
       pass
    # If DE failed, do a few Nelder-Mead random starts
    # But DE can find best. Polish best with NM.
    try:
       res = minimize(fourier_obj, bestx, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':500, 'xatol':1e-6, 'fatol':1e-9})
       if bestx is None or res.fun < bestc:
           bestx=res.x; bestc=res.fun
    except Exception:
       pass
    # If still time? no deadline. Could do one more random NM maybe.
    return bestx
```

To reduce c0, move mass from h>0.5? derivative negative where h>0.5 (since 1-2h<0), positive where h<0.5. To reduce c0 (which is sum h(1-h)), we want make h more 0/1: decrease h<0.5? ```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

FFT_L_CACHE = {}

FOURIER_B = None
FOURIER_FALLBACK = None
FOURIER_N = 0
FOURIER_L = 0
FOURIER_F = 2


def next_fast_len(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def fft_len_for_n(n):
    val = FFT_L_CACHE.get(n)
    if val is None:
        val = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        FFT_L_CACHE[n] = val
    return val


def compute_c5_direct(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def compute_c5_fft_vec(h, L=None):
    n = len(h)
    if L is None:
        L = fft_len_for_n(n)
    fh = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    fg = np.fft.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fh * fg, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c * (2.0 / n)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (diff / tot) * h

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(8):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
        else:
            idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            break
        h[idx] += diff / idx.size
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def active_lags(c, top_k=16, rel=0.002):
    mx = float(c.max())
    th = mx - max(1e-10, rel * mx)
    idx = np.where(c >= th)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    if idx.size > top_k:
        order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:top_k]
        idx = idx[order]
    return idx.astype(int)


def add_lag_gradient(g, h, m, w, n):
    if w == 0.0:
        return
    k = m - (n - 1)
    if k >= 0:
        q = k
        nv = n - q
        if nv <= 0:
            return
        g[q:n] += w * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
        g[0:nv] += -w * h[q:n]
    else:
        q = -k
        nv = n - q
        if nv <= 0:
            return
        g[0:nv] += w * (1.0 - h[q:n])
        g[q:n] += -w * h[0:nv]


def add_linear_effect(c, h, p, delta, n):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    center = n - 1
    c[center] += delta * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[p])

    if p > 0:
        c[center + 1 : center + 1 + p] += delta * (1.0 - h[p - 1::-1])
        c[center - p : center] += -delta * h[0:p]

    if n - p - 1 > 0:
        c[center + 1 : center + n - p] += -delta * h[p + 1 : n]
        c[p:center] += delta * (1.0 - h[n - 1 : p : -1])


def add_pair_updates(c, h, i, j, amt, n):
    add_linear_effect(c, h, i, -amt, n)
    add_linear_effect(c, h, j, amt, n)

    D = abs(i - j)
    amt2 = amt * amt
    center = n - 1
    c[center] -= 2.0 * amt2
    if D > 0:
        c[center + D] += amt2
        c[center - D] += amt2


def eval_pair_move(c, h, i, j, amt, n):
    if i == j or amt <= 0.0:
        return float(c.max())
    c2 = c.copy()
    add_pair_updates(c2, h, i, j, amt, n)
    return float(c2.max())


def apply_pair_move(h, c, i, j, amt, n):
    add_pair_updates(c, h, int(i), int(j), amt, n)
    h[i] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[i] - amt))
    h[j] = max(0.0, min(1.0, h[j] + amt))


def smooth_topk_obj(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    if active.size == 0:
        return 0.5
    c = compute_c5_fft_vec(x)
    vals = c[active]
    mx = vals.max()
    sh = alpha * (vals - mx)
    smx = sh.max()
    if smx > 400.0:
        val = mx + (smx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - smx)))) / alpha
    else:
        val = mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh))) / alpha
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def smooth_topk_grad(x, n, active, alpha, mu):
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    if active.size == 0:
        if mu > 0.0:
            grad += 2.0 * mu * (x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
        return grad

    c = compute_c5_fft_vec(x)
    vals = c[active]
    mx = vals.max()
    sh = alpha * (vals - mx)
    smx = sh.max()
    w = np.exp(sh - smx)
    w /= w.sum()

    for sidx in range(active.size):
        wi = w[sidx]
        if wi > 1e-14:
            add_lag_gradient(grad, x, active[sidx], wi, n)

    if mu > 0.0:
        grad += 2.0 * mu * (x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return grad


def run_active_lbfgs(h, n, deadline, top_k=16, mu=0.05):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_c5_direct(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    configs = [
        (top_k, 20.0, mu, 250),
        (top_k, 200.0, mu, 300),
        (min(40, max(16, top_k * 2)), 2000.0, 0.02, 300),
        (min(80, max(24, top_k * 3)), 20000.0, 0.01, 200),
    ]

    for tk, alpha, mu_i, itmax in configs:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 15:
            break
        c_now = compute_c5_fft_vec(best_h)
        active = active_lags(c_now, tk, 0.01)
        maxit = min(itmax, max(30, int(rem * 2)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_topk_obj,
                best_h,
                jac=smooth_topk_grad,
                args=(n, active, alpha, mu_i),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "ftol": 1e-20,
                    "gtol": 1e-16,
                    "maxfun": maxit * 10,
                    "maxcor": 60,
                    "maxls": 60,
                },
            )
            h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            c_new = compute_c5_direct(h_new)
            new_c = float(c_new.max())
            if new_c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = new_c
                best_h = h_new
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(best_h)
    return best_h, float(c.max())


def fast_pair_local(h, n, deadline, seed=1, top_k=16, cand_count=80, step0=0.05):
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = compute_c5_direct(h)
    best_c = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + n)
    step = step0
    stale = 0
    moves = 0
    iter_count = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if iter_count % 20 == 0:
            c_dir = compute_c5_direct(h)
            c = c_dir
            best_c = float(c.max())
            if best_c < 1e-16:
                break

        active = active_lags(c, top_k, 0.002)
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

        vals = c[active]
        mx = float(vals.max())
        sh = 20.0 * (vals - mx)
        smx = sh.max()
        w = np.exp(sh - smx)
        w /= w.sum()

        g = np.zeros(n)
        for sidx in range(active.size):
            if w[sidx] > 1e-14:
                add_lag_gradient(g, h, active[sidx], w[sidx], n)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        room_pos = np.where(h > 1e-8)[0]
        room_neg = np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-8)[0]
        if room_pos.size < 1 or room_neg.size < 1:
            break

        ncand = min(15, room_pos.size, room_neg.size)
        if ncand <= 0:
            break

        high = room_pos[np.argsort(g[room_pos])[::-1]][:ncand]
        low = room_neg[np.argsort(g[room_neg])[:ncand]]

        cand = []
        seen = set()

        for ii in high:
            gi = g[ii]
            if not np.isfinite(gi):
                continue
            for jj in low:
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                gj = g[jj]
                if not np.isfinite(gj):
                    continue
                key = int(ii) * n + int(jj)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                cand.append((float(gi - gj), int(ii), int(jj)))

        for _ in range(24):
            ii = int(room_pos[rng.randint(room_pos.size)])
            jj = int(room_neg[rng.randint(room_neg.size)])
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            if g[ii] is None or g[jj] is None:
                continue
            key = ii * n + jj
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            cand.append((float(g[ii] - g[jj]), ii, jj))

        if not cand:
            break

        cand.sort(reverse=True)
        if cand_count and len(cand) > cand_count:
            cand = cand[:cand_count]

        chosen = None
        chosen_c = best_c

        for _gain, ii, jj in cand:
            maxamt = min(h[ii], 1.0 - h[jj])
            if maxamt <= 1e-9:
                continue
            last_amt = -1.0
            for mult in (2.0, 1.0, 0.5):
                amt = mult * step
                if amt > maxamt:
                    amt = maxamt
                if amt <= 1e-10 or abs(amt - last_amt) <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                last_amt = amt
                trial = eval_pair_move(c, h, ii, jj, amt, n)
                if trial < chosen_c - 1e-13:
                    chosen_c = trial
                    chosen = (ii, jj, amt)
                    if chosen_c < best_c - 1e-5:
                        break
            if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                break

        if chosen is not None:
            ii, jj, amt = chosen
            maxamt = min(h[ii], 1.0 - h[jj])
            if amt > maxamt:
                amt = maxamt
            if amt > 1e-12:
                apply_pair_move(h, c, ii, jj, amt, n)
                actual = float(c.max())
                if actual < best_c:
                    best_c = actual
                    best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    best_c = actual
                moves += 1
                stale = 0
            iter_count += 1
            continue

        improved = False
        for _ in range(250):
            if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                break
            ii = int(room_pos[rng.randint(room_pos.size)])
            jj = int(room_neg[rng.randint(room_neg.size)])
            if ii == jj:
                continue
            maxamt = min(h[ii], 1.0 - h[jj])
            amt = min(maxamt, step)
            if amt <= 1e-10:
                continue
            trial = eval_pair_move(c, h, ii, jj, amt, n)
            if trial < best_c - 1e-13:
                apply_pair_move(h, c, ii, jj, amt, n)
                actual = float(c.max())
                if actual < best_c:
                    best_c = actual
                    best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    best_c = actual
                moves += 1
                improved = True
                break

        iter_count += 1
        if improved:
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            step *= 0.5
            if step < 1e-6 and stale > 3:
                break
            if step < 1e-5 and stale > 7:
                break

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_direct(h)
    cval = float(c.max())
    if cval < best_c:
        best_c = cval
        best_h = h.copy()
    else:
        best_c = cval
    return best_h, best_c


def build_fourier_basis(n, F):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    D = 2 * F + 3
    B = np.zeros((n, D), dtype=float)
    col = 0
    for m in range(1, F + 1):
        ang = np.pi * m * x
        B[:, col] = np.cos(ang)
        col += 1
        B[:, col] = np.sin(ang)
        col += 1

    t = x - 1.0
    B[:, col] = t
    col += 1
    B[:, col] = t * t
    col += 1
    B[:, col] = t * t * t
    col += 1

    for j in range(D):
        B[:, j] -= np.mean(B[:, j])

    fb = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    fb -= np.mean(fb)
    return B, fb


def prepare_fourier_basis(n, F):
    global FOURIER_B, FOURIER_FALLBACK, FOURIER_N, FOURIER_L, FOURIER_F
    FOURIER_F = F
    B, fb = build_fourier_basis(n, F)
    FOURIER_B = B
    FOURIER_FALLBACK = fb
    FOURIER_N = n
    FOURIER_L = fft_len_for_n(n)
    return B, fb


def fourier_obj(params):
    global FOURIER_B, FOURIER_FALLBACK, FOURIER_N, FOURIER_L
    if FOURIER_B is None:
        prepare_fourier_basis(1024, 2)

    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=float)
    D = 2 * FOURIER_F + 3
    coeffs = np.zeros(D)
    Lc = min(D, len(params))
    coeffs[:Lc] = params[:Lc]
    A = float(params[D]) if len(params) > D else 0.5
    A = min(1.0, max(0.0, A))

    f = FOURIER_B @ coeffs
    maxf = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if maxf < 1e-12:
        f = FOURIER_FALLBACK.copy()
        maxf = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if maxf < 1e-12:
        f = np.zeros(len(FOURIER_B))
        maxf = 1.0
    f = f / maxf

    h = 0.5 + A * f
    h = h + (0.5 * len(h) - np.sum(h)) / len(h)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    c = compute_c5_fft_vec(h, FOURIER_L)
    return float(c.max())


def run_fourier_search(seed):
    prepare_fourier_basis(2048, 2)
    F = FOURIER_F
    D = 2 * F + 3
    bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * D + [(0.25, 1.0)]

    bestx = None
    bestc = np.inf

    rs = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x0 = rs.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, size=D + 1)
    x0[-1] = 0.5
    c0 = fourier_obj(x0)
    if np.isfinite(c0):
        bestx = x0.copy()
        bestc = c0

    try:
        res = differential_evolution(
            fourier_obj,
            bounds,
            seed=seed,
            maxiter=10,
            popsize=8,
            polish=False,
            tol=1e-5,
        )
        if np.isfinite(res.fun):
            if bestx is None or res.fun < bestc:
                bestx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                bestc = float(res.fun)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if bestx is None:
        return None

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fourier_obj,
            bestx,
            method="Nelder-Mead",
            options={"maxiter": 500, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-9},
        )
        if np.isfinite(res.fun):
            if res.fun < bestc:
                bestx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                bestc = float(res.fun)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return bestx


def build_fourier_h(params, n):
    F = FOURIER_F if FOURIER_F is not None else 2
    D = 2 * F + 3
    B, fb = build_fourier_basis(n, F)

    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=float)
    coeffs = np.zeros(D)
    Lc = min(D, len(params))
    coeffs[:Lc] = params[:Lc]
    A = float(params[D]) if len(params) > D else 0.5
    A = min(1.0, max(0.0, A))

    f = B @ coeffs
    maxf = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if maxf < 1e-12:
        f = fb.copy()
        maxf = float(np.max(np.abs(f)))
    if maxf < 1e-12:
        f = np.zeros(n)
        maxf = 1.0
    f = f / maxf

    h = 0.5 + A * f
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return h


def make_upsampled(h_old, n_new):
    h_old = np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float)
    n_old = len(h_old)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(h_old.copy(), n_new)

    old_dx = 2.0 / n_old
    pos = np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) * (2.0 / n_new)
    idx = np.floor(pos / old_dx).astype(int)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n_old - 1)
    h = h_old[idx]
    return project_feasible(h, n_new)


def make_random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    rem = 0.5 * n - h.sum()
    if abs(rem) > 1e-12:
        zeros = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
        if zeros.size:
            h[zeros[0]] += rem
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_beatty_start(n, alpha_param):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    positions = set()
    for k in range(1, 4 * n + 5):
        pos = int(k * alpha_param) % n
        positions.add(pos)
        if len(positions) >= target:
            break
    for pos in positions:
        h[pos] = 1.0
    rem = 0.5 * n - h.sum()
    if abs(rem) > 1e-12:
        zeros = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
        if zeros.size:
            h[zeros[0]] += rem
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_simple_cos(n, amp=0.5):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = 0.5 + amp * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_simple_tri(n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    h = np.abs(x - 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def add_candidate(cands, h, cval):
    if h is None:
        return
    if cval is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(cval):
        return
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.ndim != 1 or h.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return
    cands.append((float(cval), int(h.size), len(cands), h))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1.0
    best_n = 0
    cands = []

    try:
        init = initial_h_values
    except Exception:
        init = None

    if init is not None:
        init = np.asarray(init, dtype=float)
        if init.ndim == 1 and init.size > 0:
            n0 = int(init.size)
            if n0 > 2500:
                h0 = make_upsampled(init, 2048)
                add_candidate(cands, h0, compute_c5_direct(h0))
            else:
                h0 = project_feasible(init.copy(), n0)
                add_candidate(cands, h0, compute_c5_direct(h0))

                hm = 0.5 * (h0 + h0[::-1])
                hm = project_feasible(hm, n0)
                add_candidate(cands, hm, compute_c5_direct(hm))

                for nt in (1024, 2048):
                    if nt != n0:
                        hup = make_upsampled(h0, nt)
                        add_candidate(cands, hup, compute_c5_direct(hup))
                        hm2 = 0.5 * (hup + hup[::-1])
                        hm2 = project_feasible(hm2, nt)
                        add_candidate(cands, hm2, compute_c5_direct(hm2))

    if not cands:
        nbase = 1024
        add_candidate(cands, make_simple_cos(nbase), compute_c5_direct(make_simple_cos(nbase)))
        add_candidate(cands, make_simple_tri(nbase), compute_c5_direct(make_simple_tri(nbase)))
        add_candidate(cands, make_random_binary(nbase, rng), compute_c5_direct(make_random_binary(nbase, rng)))
        add_candidate(cands, make_beatty_start(nbase, 0.618), compute_c5_direct(make_beatty_start(nbase, 0.618)))

    if time.time() < deadline - 350.0:
        params = run_fourier_search(seed + 101)
        if params is not None:
            for nt in (1024, 2048):
                hf = build_fourier_h(params, nt)
                cf = compute_c5_direct(hf)
                if cf < 0.45:
                    add_candidate(cands, hf, cf)
                hf2 = 0.5 * (hf + hf[::-1])
                hf2 = project_feasible(hf2, nt)
                cf2 = compute_c5_direct(hf2)
                if cf2 < 0.45:
                    add_candidate(cands, hf2, cf2)

    cands.sort()

    seen_n = {}
    top_cands = []
    for cval, nn, _, hh in cands:
        if nn in seen_n:
            if cval < seen_n[nn]:
                seen_n[nn] = cval
                for k in range(len(top_cands)):
                    pass
            continue
        seen_n[nn] = cval
        top_cands.append((cval, nn, hh))
        if len(top_cands) >= 4:
            break

    top_cands.sort(key=None)

    for rank, (c0, n0, h0) in enumerate(top_cands):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 160.0:
            break

        if rank == 0:
            alloc = min(rem - 50.0, rem * 0.58)
        elif rank == 1:
            alloc = min(rem - 50.0, rem * 0.42)
        else:
            alloc = min(rem - 50.0, rem * 0.30)

        if alloc < 60.0:
            alloc = min(rem - 40.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.25))

        t_start = time.time()

        lbfg_deadline = t_start + max(20.0, alloc * 0.22)
        h1, c1 = run_active_lbfgs(h0, n0, lbfg_deadline, top_k=16)

        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1
            best_n = n0

        pair_deadline = t_start + max(lbfg_deadline, t_start + alloc * 0.80)
        h2, c2 = fast_pair_local(h1, n0, pair_deadline, seed=seed + 7 + n0)

        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
            best_n = n0

        rem2 = deadline - time.time()
        if rem2 > 50.0:
            pol_deadline = min(deadline - 10.0, t_start + alloc * 0.98)
            if pol_deadline > time.time() + 10.0:
                ph, pc = run_active_lbfgs(h2, n0, min(pol_deadline, time.time() + max(20.0, alloc * 0.12)), top_k=24)
                if pc < best_c:
                    best_c = pc
                    best_h = ph
                    best_n = n0

        rem3 = deadline - time.time()
        if rem3 > 40.0:
            final_pair_deadline = min(deadline - 8.0, t_start + alloc)
            if final_pair_deadline > time.time() + 10.0:
                fh, fc = fast_pair_local(best_h if best_n == n0 else h2, n0, final_pair_deadline, seed=seed + 17 + n0)
                if fc < best_c:
                    best_c = fc
                    best_h = fh
                    best_n = n0

    if best_h is None:
        nfallback = 1024
        best_h = make_simple_tri(nfallback)
        best_c = float(compute_c5_direct(best_h).max())
        best_n = nfallback

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 90.0:
        hh, cc = fast_pair_local(best_h, best_n, deadline - 4.0, seed=seed + 99, step0=0.03)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_h = hh
            best_c = cc
            best_n = best_n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = float(compute_c5_direct(best_h).max())

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
